Better O(n) delta. Let's define h values 0/1. Let a = index to set 1? Move a 0 at b to 1 and a 1 at a to 0. We can compute the change in all c_l. For each lag l with orientation positive (c_l = ∑_{i=0}^{n-l-1} h[i] (1-h[i+l])) and negative (c_{-l} = ∑_{i=0}^{n-l-1} h[i+l](1-h[i]))? np.correlate includes both but we can maintain both arrays counts. Since h binary, c_l counts source 0 target? Let's define for l≥0:
P_l = ∑_{i=0}^{n-l-1} h_i (1 - h_{i+l})  (1 at i, 0 at i+l)
N_l = ∑_{i=0}^{n-l-1} h_{i+l} (1 - h_i)  (1 at i+l, 0 at i)
Full correlation maximum of max(P_l,N_l) for l=0..n-1. For l=0, P_0=0 for binary? h_i(1-h_i)=0.
When changing h at positions x,y (x old1->0, y old0->1), only terms involving these indices in pairs with separation l change. For P_l:
Term T_i = h_i (1-h_{i+l}). If x,y pair:
- if y - x = l: T_x (i=x) changes: old 1*(1-1? h_y old0? Wait old h_x=1,h_y=0, T_x old =1*(1-0)=1. New h_x=0,h_y=1, T_x new=0. Delta -1. Also T_y? i=y? requires y+l within: not if y+x? For pair y = x+l, T_y old=0*(1-h_{x+2l})=0, new=1*(1-h_{x+2l}) = 1 - h_{x+2l}. Delta + (1 - h_{x+2l}) if valid. Also if x - y = l: T_y old =1? (h_y old0 no) wait if x = y+l. Need general. Simpler O(n) recompute affected lags by scanning all i where i or i+l is x/y. For each l, terms affected if x in [0,L) as i and x+l in [0,n) OR y similarly. There are at most 4 terms per l. We can compute delta by old/new values for those. Do for l=0..n-1 O(n) with O(1). Good.

Binary local hill-climb with recompute:
```
def binary_local(h, n, max_evals, rng):
  h=h.copy().astype(float)
  c5=compute_c5(h)
  ones=np.flatnonzero(h>0.5); zeros=np.flatnonzero(h<0.5)
  evals=0
  for _ in range(max_evals):
    improved=False
    # Try a few random swaps
    for _ in range(20):
       x=rng.choice(ones); y=rng.choice(zeros)
       h[x]=0; h[y]=1
       c_try=compute_c5(h)
       if c_try < c5 - 1e-15:
          c5=c_try; updated ones/zeros; improved=True; break
       else: h[x]=1; h[y]=0
    if not improved:
       # Try targeted max lag pairs? maybe skip
       break
```
Need update ones/zeros after accepted. To target, compute max lag from c: maybe identify a bad pair and swap. We can do:
- c = np.correlate(...). max index. Determine lag k = idx - (n-1). If k>0 (N_l: later 1 early 0), bad pairs are i with h[i+l]=1,h[i]=0. Swapping them (set i=1, i+l=0) would reduce N_l? Actually N_l counts later1 early0; to reduce, either turn later to0 or early to1. Swap endpoints: i becomes1, i+l becomes0, term becomes 0? New: early1, late0; N_l term =0? For N_l (late1, early0) new late0, so delta -1. It may affect other lags. If k<0 (P_l: early1 late0), bad pairs i with h[i]=1,h[i+l]=0; swap to 0,1 reduces P_l. So for max lag, collect up to maybe 50 bad pairs, try swapping few.

def slsqp_smooth(n, h0, betas, max_iters):
    global _SMOOTH_BETA, _TARGET_SUM, _N_POINTS
    _N_POINTS=n; _TARGET_SUM=n/2.0
    best_h=h0; best_c=compute_c5(h0)
    cur=h0
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_sum,'jac':constraint_sum_jac}]
    for beta in betas:
       if time... break
       _SMOOTH_BETA=beta
       res=minimize(smooth_obj, cur, jac=smooth_gradient, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints, options={'maxiter':max_iters,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
       if res.success? or res.fun finite:
          h2=make_feasible(res.x,n)
          c2=compute_c5(h2)
          if c2<best_c: best...
          cur=make_feasible(res.x,n) # even if not improved? maybe
    return best_h,best_c
```

def local_binary(h0, n, rng, max_trials):
    h=h0.astype(float).copy()
    c5=compute_c5(h)
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h>0.5)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h<0.5)
    trials=0
    for it in range(max_trials):
       if time... break
       improved=False
       # get max lag to target
       c_counts = np.correlate(h.astype(np.int8), (1-h).astype(np.int8), mode='full')
       idx = int(np.argmax(c_counts))
       lag = idx - (n-1)
       tried=0
       while tried < 20:
          pair=None
          if lag > 0: # N_lag: pairs i with h[i]=0,h[i+lag]=1
              i = np.random... maybe choose random from bad indices

          h[x]=0; h[y]=1; c_try=compute_c5(h)
          if c_try < c5 - 1e-15:
             update ones zeros; c5=c_try; improved=True; break
          else:
             h[x]=oldx; h[y]=oldy; tried+=1
       if not improved:
          # random pairs 5
          ...
          if no improve: break
    return h,c5

Let's improve make_feasible:
```
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if len(h)!=n: h=ffill? assume
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
       else: mask = h > 1e-12
       cnt=np.count_nonzero(mask)
       if cnt==0: break
       h[mask] += diff/cnt
       h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
For binary sum integer, no change.

Potential floating sum: For n even, target integer; if we set exactly half ones, sum exact as float integer <=2^53. Good. For continuous, maybe abs <1e-12; but make_feasible leaves maybe 1e-12. Assert <1e-8.

Smooth gradient vectorization details:
```
def smooth_gradient(x):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    beta = _SMOOTH_BETA
    m = np.max(c)
    e = np.exp(beta*(c-m))
    w = e / e.sum()
    n = len(x)
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    l0 = n-1
    # k=0
    grad += (1.0 - 2.0*x) * w[l0]
    for l in range(1,n):
       wp = w[l0 + l]
       if wp > 1e-300:
          early = x[:-l]
          late = x[l:]
          grad[:-l] -= late * wp
          grad[l:] += (1.0 - early) * wp
       wn = w[l0 - l]
       if wn > 1e-300:
          early = x[:-l]
          late = x[l:]
          grad[:-l] += (1.0 - late) * wn
          grad[l:] -= early * wn
    return grad
```
Check for l=n-1, slices length1. Good. Sum gradient? Constraint handled. For binary and positive lag: w_l> maybe; gradient for that lag: early term -=late, late +=(1-early). If pair 0,1 (N_l) early0 late1; grad late +=1, early -=1. Minimizing F decreases c by moving mass from late? To reduce N_l, we want reduce late and increase early; gradient positive at late (increase objective if increase late), negative at early (increase if decrease early), so descent subtract grad: decrease late, increase early. Good.

SLSQP may call gradient at bounds; no issue.

Could `np.exp(beta*(c-m))` under/overflow? c-m≤0, β up to 1000, min exp -1000 underflows to0; sum okay. If c max large plateau, weights. Use `np.errstate`? Not needed but can.

Potential performance of smooth_gradient: loop over l up to n, each with four slice operations; for n=480 ~480*4* slices; SLSQP maybe many. Could optimize by only considering weights above threshold (β large, only max lag nonzero). We can threshold weights <1e-12 skip; use if. But still loops. Could build list of active lags where w>1e-10. For β large, few. For β small, many but maxiter small. Implement:
```
active = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-8)
for idx in active:
   if idx == n-1: ...
   else: l=abs(idx-(n-1)); ...
```
This may skip many. But if β=20, many weights. n small. We'll use active to save.
For each active idx:
```
if idx > n-1: l=idx-(n-1); wp=...; early=x[:-l]; late=x[l:]
elif idx < n-1: l=n-1-idx; wn=...; early=x[:-l]; late=x[l:]
```
Note if multiple lags? Each output index unique. Good. This loops active count. But vector operations allocate slices (views) and temporaries. okay.

Function:
```
def make_continuous_fourier(n, modes, amps, phases, beta):
    i = np.arange(n)
    u = np.zeros(n)
    for r,a,ph in zip(modes,amps,phases):
        u += a*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*r*i/n + ph)
    if beta > 0: v=np.tanh(beta*u)
    else: v=u
    v -= v.mean()
    m = max(float(np.max(np.abs(v))),1e-15)
    if m>1: v/=m
    v=np.clip(v,-1,1)
    h=0.5+0.5*v
    return make_feasible(h,n)
```
This sequence even? u sum of cos, but after tanh and mean, not exactly even? tanh(sum cos) is even if phases 0? If phases not 0, not even. Sum zero enforced. B may be O(1) but maybe. For continuous grid, phase maybe 0 only? Use phases 0 for all to preserve even boundary. But translation can help? Not necessary.

Maybe continuous parameter with r=1,20, tanh beta large, a ~0.5, after mean/scaling. This approximates sign but with mean corrections. Could be better than binary because mean bias handled. Include.

Potential use of `scipy.optimize.differential_evolution` for continuous parameters could optimize a and phase. But global grid enough.

def make_binary_fourier_theta(theta, n, a, r, phi, roll, cos_theta=None):
    if cos_theta is None: cos_theta=np.cos(theta)
    u=cos_theta + a*np.cos(r*theta + phi)
    if a==0: u=cos_theta
    idx=np.argsort(u, kind='mergesort')[::-1][:n//2]
    h=np.zeros(n); h[idx]=1.0
    if roll: h=np.roll(h, roll)
    return h

Detailed functions:

def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0/n))
```

`slsqp_smooth`:
```
def slsqp_smooth(n, h0, betas, max_iterations):
    global _SMOOTH_BETA, _TARGET_SUM
    h0 = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(h0)
    cur = h0.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0) for i in range(n)]
    constraints = [{'type':'eq','fun':constraint_sum,'jac':constraint_sum_jac}]
    for beta in betas:
        _SMOOTH_BETA = float(beta)
        try:
           res = minimize(smooth_obj, cur, jac=smooth_grad, method='SLSQP',
                          bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints,
                          options={'maxiter':max_iterations,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
           if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
              h2=make_feasible(res.x,n)
              c2=compute_c5(h2)
              if c2 < best_c:
                 best_c=c2; best_h=h2
              # continue from h2 even if not improved? maybe if feasible
              cur = h2
        except Exception:
           pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
Potential SLSQP with equality and analytic gradient: It may modify cur in-place? We pass copy.
Need set `_TARGET_SUM = n/2.0` before. Add parameter or set in function. Good.

```
s = e.sum()
if s == 0:
   w=np.zeros_like(c); w[c == m]=1.0/count
else: w=e/s
```

Potential objective large; SLSQP ftol on scaled objective. Counts up to n, ok.

`local_binary_search`:
```
def local_binary_search(h0,n,rng,deadline,max_evals):
    h=np.asarray(h0,dtype=float).copy()
    if not is_binary_h(h): return h, compute_c5(h)
    c5=compute_c5(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    current=c5
    T=0.003
    Tend=1e-5
    evals=0
    for step in range(max_evals):
       if time.time() > deadline or evals >= max_evals: break
       T = T * (Tend/T)**(1.0/max(max_evals-1,1))
       moves=[]
       # targeted: compute corr counts every step maybe expensive but okay
       if step % 2 == 0:
          h_int = h.astype(np.int8)
          corr = np.correlate(h_int, (1 - h_int).astype(np.int8), mode='full')
          idx = int(np.argmax(corr))
          lag = idx - (n-1)
          if lag > 0:
              bad = (h[:-lag] == 0) & (h[lag:] == 1)
              if np.any(bad):
                  pos = np.flatnonzero(bad)
                  choices = rng.choice(pos, size=min(3, len(pos)), replace=False)
                  for p in choices:
                     early = int(p); late = int(p+lag)
                     moves.append((early, late))
          elif lag < 0:
              l = -lag
              bad = (h[:-l] == 1) & (h[l:] == 0)
              ... early=p, late=p+l
       # random moves
       ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
       zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
       if len(ones)>0 and len(zeros)>0:
          rnd = min(3, len(ones), len(zeros))
          for _ in range(rnd):
             a=int(rng.choice(ones)); b=int(rng.choice(zeros)); moves.append((a,b))
       improved=False
       for (a,b) in moves:
          if a==b: continue
          if evals >= max_evals or time.time()>deadline: break
          olda=float(h[a]); oldb=float(h[b])
          if olda == oldb: continue
          h[a]=oldb; h[b]=olda  # swap (binary)
          evals +=1
          c_try=compute_c5(h)
          if c_try < current - 1e-15:
             current=c_try
             improved=True
          else:
             # SA accept
             delta = c_try - best_c
             if delta < 0: improved=True
             elif delta < 1e-12 or (T > 0 and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / max(T,1e-9))):
                current = c_try
             else:
                h[a]=olda; h[b]=oldb; continue
          # after accepted (whether improve or SA)
          if c_try < best_c:
             best_c=c_try; best_h=h.copy()
          # Do not break? Could try another move after improvement? We can break to avoid too many per step.
          # maybe break to one move per step
          break
       if not improved:
          # optional continue with worse current? If no accept, break after some no-progress
          # We'll break if evals small? Rather continue with SA can accept, improved flag only current improve. If accepted worse, improved False but current changed. To escape, allow some rounds.
          # We can track no_improve_count if best not improved; simple if evals > 500 and no best improve for 100 steps break? Keep simple: if no best improve in 200 evals, break.
       if best_c == current and not improved:
          # if current equals best and no accept better, maybe try random only? Could break to avoid plateaus.
          if evals > 100:
             # maybe continue limited
             pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need avoid infinite stuck. Simpler: make SA current, accept bad moves frequently at start, so improved may false but current changes. We can count best_improve_evals; if no best improvement for e.g. 200 evals, reduce T or break.

```

Now code details for `make_binary_from_parts` with argpartition:
```
def make_binary_from_parts(theta, cos_theta, n, r, a, phi, roll):
    u = cos_theta
    if a != 0.0:
        u = u + a*np.cos(r*theta + phi)
    k = n // 2
    idx = np.argpartition(-u, k)[:k]
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[idx] = 1.0
    if roll:
        h = np.roll(h, int(roll))
    return h
```
If k=0? n>2. For a=0, -u top half. Good.

Continuous candidate function:
```
def make_continuous_from_parts(theta, n, r, a, beta, phi, scale_to_bounds=True):
    u = np.cos(theta) + a*np.cos(r*theta + phi)
    if beta > 0: v=np.tanh(beta*u)
    else: v=u
    v -= v.mean()
    m = np.max(np.abs(v))
    if m > 1.0: v /= m
    # If max <1, we could scale up to 1 to increase amplitude? Yes if m<1, v/=m to use full range. But scaling up increases mean zero preserved if scale. If m>0, v/=m. This makes max abs 1. But if distribution asymmetric, after mean subtraction maxpos != maxneg; scaling by maxabs feasible. So always v /= m if m>1e-12.
    v = np.clip(v, -1.0, 1.0)
    h=0.5+0.5*v
    return make_feasible(h,n)
```
Scaling after mean zero preserves zero. Good.

If beta large, v nearly sign, after mean subtract/scaling, h close binary but not exact; make_feasible adjust.

Could use `mode='square'` beta=100 effectively binary with threshold? But top-half binary better.

Now, maybe continuous with scaling to max could create values outside if one side after mean? We scale by max of abs after mean, so within.

def is_binary_h(h):

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_SMOOTH_BETA = 50.0
_TARGET_SUM = 0.0


def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float('inf')
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h_tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        m = min(n, h.size)
        if m > 0:
            h_tmp[:m] = h[:m]
        h = h_tmp
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(1000):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-12)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt == 0:
            break
        h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def interpolate_to_n(h_src, n_dst):
    if h_src is None or len(h_src) == 0:
        return np.zeros(n_dst, dtype=float)
    if len(h_src) == n_dst:
        return np.array(h_src, dtype=float).copy()
    if len(h_src) < 2:
        return np.full(n_dst, float(h_src[0]))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(h_src), endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_dst, endpoint=False)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_src)
    return out


def constraint_sum(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _TARGET_SUM)


def constraint_sum_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(x)


def smooth_obj(x):
    beta = _SMOOTH_BETA
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = beta * (c - m)
    # At the maximum lag this is zero, so at least one entry is 1.
    val = np.max(shifted, axis=0)
    if val > 20.0:
        shifted = shifted - val
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return float(m + (1.0 / beta) * math.log(s))


def smooth_grad(x):
    beta = _SMOOTH_BETA
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full')
    m = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = beta * (c - m)
    val = np.max(shifted, axis=0)
    if val > 20.0:
        shifted = shifted - val
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        nm = int(np.count_nonzero(c == m))
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[c == m] = 1.0 / max(nm, 1)
    else:
        w = e / s

    n = len(x)
    center = n - 1
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    active = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-12)
    for idx in active:
        wi = float(w[idx])
        if idx == center:
            grad += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        elif idx > center:
            lag = idx - center
            early = x[:-lag]
            late = x[lag:]
            grad[:-lag] -= late * wi
            grad[lag:] += (1.0 - early) * wi
        else:
            lag = center - idx
            early = x[:-lag]
            late = x[lag:]
            grad[:-lag] += (1.0 - late) * wi
            grad[lag:] -= early * wi
    return grad


def slsqp_smooth(n, h0, betas, max_iterations):
    global _SMOOTH_BETA, _TARGET_SUM
    h0 = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h0)

    cur = h0.copy()
    _TARGET_SUM = 0.5 * n
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': constraint_sum, 'jac': constraint_sum_jac}]

    for beta in betas:
        _SMOOTH_BETA = float(beta)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_obj,
                cur,
                jac=smooth_grad,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': max_iterations, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'iprint': 0}
            )
            if hasattr(res, 'x') and hasattr(res.x, '__len__'):
                if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                    h2 = make_feasible(res.x, n)
                    c2 = compute_c5(h2)
                    if c2 < best_c:
                        best_c = c2
                        best_h = h2
                    cur = h2
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c


def make_binary_from_parts(theta, cos_theta, n, r, a, phi, roll):
    u = cos_theta
    if a != 0.0:
        u = u + a * np.cos(r * theta + phi)
    k = int(n // 2)
    if k <= 0:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(-u, k)[:k]
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    if roll:
        h = np.roll(h, int(roll))
    return h


def make_binary_two_tone(theta, cos_theta, n, r1, a1, r2, a2, roll):
    u = cos_theta
    if a1 != 0.0:
        u = u + a1 * np.cos(r1 * theta)
    if a2 != 0.0:
        u = u + a2 * np.cos(r2 * theta)
    k = int(n // 2)
    if k <= 0:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(-u, k)[:k]
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    if roll:
        h = np.roll(h, int(roll))
    return h


def wienedemann_h(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    half = n // 2
    for i in range(n):
        if ((i * (i + 1)) // 2) % n < half:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def random_binary_h(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n < 2:
        return h
    u = rng.rand(n)
    k = n // 2
    idx = np.argpartition(-u, k)[:k]
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def make_continuous_from_parts(theta, n, r, a, beta, phi):
    u = np.cos(theta) + a * np.cos(r * theta + phi)
    if beta > 0.0:
        v = np.tanh(beta * u)
    else:
        v = u
    v -= float(np.mean(v))
    m = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
    if m > 1e-14:
        v = v / m
    v = np.clip(v, -1.0, 1.0)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * v
    return make_feasible(h, n)


def is_binary_h(h):
    r = np.round(h)
    return bool(np.all(np.abs(h - r) < 1e-6))


def random_continuous_h(n, rng):
    h = rng.rand(n)
    return make_feasible(h, n)


def update_best(old_h, old_c, old_n, h, n, c):
    if c < old_c and math.isfinite(c):
        if h is not None:
            return np.array(h, dtype=float).copy(), float(c), int(n)
    return old_h, old_c, old_n


def add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c, max_keep=120):
    if not math.isfinite(c):
        return
    cand_list.append((float(c), h, int(n)))
    while len(cand_list) > max_keep:
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
            if cand_list[i][0] > cand_list[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        del cand_list[worst]


def select_top(cand_list, k=20):
    selected = []
    working = list(cand_list)
    for _ in range(min(int(k), len(working))):
        best_i = 0
        for i in range(1, len(working)):
            if working[i][0] < working[best_i][0]:
                best_i = i
        selected.append(working[best_i])
        del working[best_i]
    return selected


def local_binary_search(h0, n, rng, deadline, max_evals):
    h = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float).copy()
    if n < 4:
        return h, compute_c5(h)
    if not is_binary_h(h):
        return h, compute_c5(h)

    current = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = current

    ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
    if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
        return h, current

    T = 0.0035
    Tend = 1e-5
    stall_limit = max(350, max_evals // 2)
    no_improve = 0
    evals = 0

    for step in range(max_evals):
        if time.time() > deadline or evals >= max_evals or no_improve > stall_limit:
            break
        T = T * (Tend / max(T, 1e-12)) ** (1.0 / max(max_evals - 1, 1))

        moves = []

        if n <= 240 and step % 3 == 0:
            try:
                hi = h.astype(np.int8)
                li = (1 - h).astype(np.int8)
                corr = np.correlate(hi, li, mode='full')
                idx = int(np.argmax(corr))
                lag = idx - (n - 1)
                if lag > 0:
                    bad = (h[:-lag] < 0.5) & (h[lag:] > 0.5)
                    if np.any(bad):
                        pos = np.flatnonzero(bad)
                        take = min(4, len(pos))
                        chosen = rng.choice(pos, size=take, replace=False)
                        for p in chosen:
                            moves.append((int(p), int(p + lag)))
                elif lag < 0:
                    ll = -lag
                    bad = (h[:-ll] > 0.5) & (h[ll:] < 0.5)
                    if np.any(bad):
                        pos = np.flatnonzero(bad)
                        take = min(4, len(pos))
                        chosen = rng.choice(pos, size=take, replace=False)
                        for p in chosen:
                            moves.append((int(p), int(p + ll)))
            except Exception:
                pass

        ones = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5)
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5)
        if len(ones) > 0 and len(zeros) > 0:
            rndn = min(4, len(ones), len(zeros))
            for _ in range(rndn):
                a = int(rng.choice(ones))
                b = int(rng.choice(zeros))
                moves.append((a, b))

        improved_any = False
        for a, b in moves:
            if time.time() > deadline or evals >= max_evals:
                break
            if a == b:
                continue
            ia = int(a)
            ib = int(b)
            olda = float(h[ia])
            oldb = float(h[ib])
            if abs(olda - oldb) < 1e-12:
                continue

            h[ia] = oldb
            h[ib] = olda
            evals += 1
            c_try = compute_c5(h)

            if c_try < current - 1e-15:
                current = c_try
                improved_any = True
            else:
                delta = c_try - best_c
                if delta >= 0.0:
                    accept = (delta < 1e-12)
                    if not accept and T > 1e-12:
                        try:
                            accept = (rng.random() < math.exp(-max(delta, 0.0) / max(T, 1e-12)))
                        except Exception:
                            accept = False
                    if not accept:
                        h[ia] = olda
                        h[ib] = oldb
                        no_improve += 1
                        continue
                current = c_try

            if c_try < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c_try
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                improved_any = True
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            # one accepted move per outer step; keeps the search steady
            break

        if not improved_any and no_improve > stall_limit:
            break

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    np.random.seed(int(seed))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    start_time = time.time()
    end_time = start_time + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')
    best_n = 0

    cand_list = []

    def local_update(h, n, c):
        nonlocal best_h, best_c, best_n
        best_h, best_c, best_n = update_best(best_h, best_c, best_n, h, n, c)

    # Parent construction, if available.
    try:
        hp = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if hp.size > 1:
            hp = make_feasible(hp, hp.size)
            cp = compute_c5(hp)
            local_update(hp, hp.size, cp)
            add_candidate(cand_list, hp, hp.size, cp)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Ensure we always have something.
    if best_n == 0:
        n0 = 160
        theta0 = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n0) / n0
        h0 = make_binary_from_parts(theta0, np.cos(theta0), n0, 10, 0.6, 0.0, 0)
        c0 = compute_c5(h0)
        local_update(h0, n0, c0)
        add_candidate(cand_list, h0, n0, c0)

    gen_deadline = max(start_time + 20.0, end_time - 180.0)

    # Coarse structured binary search.
    n_list = [160, 320, 480]
    if end_time - start_time > 700:
        n_list.append(640)

    r_all = [8, 10, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 60, 80]
    a_list = [0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.9, 1.3, 1.8, 2.6]

    for n in n_list:
        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
            break
        if n % 2 != 0:
            n += 1
        theta = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n) / n
        cos_theta = np.cos(theta)

        r_cands = []
        for r in r_all:
            if n % r == 0 and 0 < r <= max(4, n // 4):
                r_cands.append(int(r))
        if not r_cands:
            r_cands = [max(1, n // 10)]

        rolls = [0, 1, 2, max(1, n // 16), max(2, n // 8), max(4, n // 4)]
        rolls = sorted(set(int(x) % n for x in rolls))

        for r in r_cands:
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            for a in a_list:
                if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                    break
                for rr in rolls:
                    h = make_binary_from_parts(theta, cos_theta, n, r, a, 0.0, rr)
                    c = compute_c5(h)
                    local_update(h, n, c)
                    add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)
                    if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                        break

        # Independent phase for the high tone, modest subset.
        phase_r = r_cands[:3]
        for r in phase_r:
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            for a in [0.5, 1.0, 1.6]:
                for phi in [0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, np.pi, 1.5 * np.pi]:
                    for rr in [0, max(1, n // 8), max(2, n // 4)]:
                        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                            break
                        h = make_binary_from_parts(theta, cos_theta, n, r, a, phi, rr)
                        c = compute_c5(h)
                        local_update(h, n, c)
                        add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

        # Two-tone binary candidates, very small set.
        if time.time() < gen_deadline - 60:
            for r1 in r_cands[:3]:
                if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                    break
                for r2 in [2 * r1, 4 * r1]:
                    if r2 >= n or n % r2 != 0:
                        continue
                    for a1 in [0.4, 0.9]:
                        for a2 in [0.3, 0.7]:
                            for rr in [0, max(1, n // 8)]:
                                if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                                    break
                                h = make_binary_two_tone(theta, cos_theta, n, r1, a1, r2, a2, rr)
                                c = compute_c5(h)
                                local_update(h, n, c)
                                add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

    # Continuous tanh/Fourier candidates.
    for n in [160, 320, 480]:
        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
            break
        theta = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n) / n
        for r in [8, 10, 16, 20, 32, 40]:
            if n % r != 0 or r > n // 2:
                continue
            for a in [0.4, 0.7, 1.1, 1.7]:
                for beta in [1.0, 2.5, 6.0]:
                    for phi in [0.0, 0.5 * np.pi]:
                        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                            break
                        h = make_continuous_from_parts(theta, n, r, a, beta, phi)
                        c = compute_c5(h)
                        local_update(h, n, c)
                        add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

    # Deterministic and random additional starts.
    for n in [160, 320, 480]:
        if time.time() > gen_deadline:
            break
        h = wienedemann_h(n)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        local_update(h, n, c)
        add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

        for _ in range(3):
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            h = random_binary_h(n, rng)
            c = compute_c5(h)
            local_update(h, n, c)
            add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() > gen_deadline:
                break
            h = random_continuous_h(n, rng)
            c = compute_c5(h)
            local_update(h, n, c)
            add_candidate(cand_list, h, n, c)

    selected = select_top(cand_list, 24)

    opt_deadline = max(start_time + 30.0, end_time - 45.0)

    # Refine selected candidates.
    for k, item in enumerate(selected):
        if time.time() > opt_deadline:
            break
        c, h, n = item
        h = make_feasible(h, n)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        local_update(h, n, c)

        # A few local binary refinements for very good binary starts.
        if k < 5 and is_binary_h(h):
            if n <= 200:
                max_evals = 3500
            elif n <= 384:
                max_evals = 1400
            else:
                max_evals = 500
            if time.time() < opt_deadline - 15:
                hl, cl = local_binary_search(h, n, rng, min(opt_deadline - 20, end_time - 60), max_evals)
                hl = make_feasible(hl, n)
                cl = compute_c5(hl)
                local_update(hl, n, cl)
                if cl < c:
                    h, c = hl, cl

        # Smooth SLSQP refinement.
        if n <= 512 and time.time() < opt_deadline - 30:
            if n <= 256:
                betas = (30.0, 150.0, 500.0)
                iters = 240
            else:
                betas = (30.0, 150.0)
                iters = 120
            hs, cs = slsqp_smooth(n, h, betas, iters)
            hs = make_feasible(hs, n)
            cs = compute_c5(hs)
            local_update(hs, n, cs)
            if cs < c:
                h, c = hs, cs

        # For exceptionally promising small solutions, try another SLSQP pass.
        if k < 2 and c < 0.382 and n <= 320 and time.time() < opt_deadline - 35:
            hs2, cs2 = slsqp_smooth(n, h, (60.0, 300.0, 900.0), 320)
            hs2 = make_feasible(hs2, n)
            cs2 = compute_c5(hs2)
            local_update(hs2, n, cs2)
            if cs2 < c:
                h, c = hs2, cs2

    # Use the best found construction to seed other resolutions if time remains.
    if best_h is not None:
        resolutions = [160, 320, 480, 640]
        if best_n not in resolutions:
            resolutions.append(best_n)
        resolutions = sorted(set(resolutions))
        for n2 in resolutions:
            if time.time() > end_time - 80:
                break
            if n2 == best_n:
                continue
            h2 = interpolate_to_n(best_h, n2)
            h2 = make_feasible(h2, n2)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            local_update(h2, n2, c2)
            if c2 <= best_c + 0.003 and n2 <= 512 and time.time() < end_time - 60:
                if n2 <= 320:
                    hs2, cs2 = slsqp_smooth(n2, h2, (40.0, 220.0), 180)
                else:
                    hs2, cs2 = slsqp_smooth(n2, h2, (40.0, 220.0), 100)
                hs2 = make_feasible(hs2, n2)
                cs2 = compute_c5(hs2)
                local_update(hs2, n2, cs2)

    # Final aggressive polish if still near the target and there is time.
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < end_time - 50 and best_c > 0.380:
        if best_n <= 320:
            hf, cf = slsqp_smooth(best_n, best_h, (80.0, 350.0, 1200.0), 400)
            hf = make_feasible(hf, best_n)
            cf = compute_c5(hf)
            local_update(hf, best_n, cf)

    if best_h is not None and is_binary_h(best_h) and time.time() < end_time - 35:
        ml = local_binary_search(best_h, best_n, rng, end_time - 25, 2500)
        local_update(ml[0], best_n, ml[1])

    # Final feasibility cleanup.
    if best_h is None:
        n0 = 160
        theta0 = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n0) / n0
        best_h = make_binary_from_parts(theta0, np.cos(theta0), n0, 10, 0.6, 0.0, 0)
        best_n = n0
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    old_h = np.array(best_h, dtype=float).copy()
    old_c = float(best_c)
    old_n = int(best_n)

    fin_h = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    fin_c = compute_c5(fin_h)
    if math.isfinite(fin_c) and fin_c <= old_c + 1e-12:
        best_h, best_c, best_n = fin_h, fin_c, best_n
    else:
        best_h, best_c, best_n = old_h, old_c, old_n

    best_h = np.clip(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_n = int(len(best_h))

    # Very last safety repair, preserving the better of repaired/original.
    repaired = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    rc = compute_c5(repaired)
    if math.isfinite(rc) and rc <= best_c + 1e-12:
        best_h = repaired
        best_c = rc

    if not math.isfinite(best_c) or best_c > 0.9:
        # Fallback to parent or simple admissible binary if everything failed.
        try:
            if initial_h_values is not None:
                fb = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
                fb = make_feasible(fb, len(fb))
                best_h = fb
                best_n = len(fb)
                best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
        except Exception:
            n0 = 160
            theta0 = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n0) / n0
            best_h = make_binary_from_parts(theta0, np.cos(theta0), n0, 10, 0.6, 0.0, 0)
            best_n = n0
            best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    return (best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n))
```
